# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 0,
  "instanceId": "example",
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeColor


## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, or one emoji/icon grapheme, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"👩‍💻"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "observedAt": 6,
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 6
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "availability": "logged_out",
  "blockedUntil": null,
  "id": "example",
  "installationId": "example",
  "instanceId": "example",
  "label": "example",
  "loginRevision": "example",
  "provider": "codex",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_in",
    "blockers": {
      "limitError": {
        "remainingPercent": 0,
        "resetsAt": 2,
        "usedPercent": 5,
        "windowDurationMins": 1
      }
    },
    "cursorSdkAuth": {
      "source": "environment",
      "status": "logged-in"
    },
    "observedAt": 3,
    "usage": {
      "costUsd": 8,
      "inputTokens": 0
    },
    "windows": {}
  },
  "signedInAs": "example"
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": true,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "legacy",
  "mcp": "unavailable",
  "modeSelection": true,
  "modelSelection": true,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

```json
      "runId": "example",
      "type": "turn"
    }

    "instanceId": "example",
    "nativeId": "example",
    "provider": "opencode"
  },
  "origin": "root",
  "parentId": "example",
  "status": {
    "error": {
      "kind": "quota",
      "message": "example"
    },
    "state": "failed"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

## BrowserOrigin

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOrigin.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An exact canonical HTTP(S) origin, including scheme and optional port, with no path, query, fragment or URL credentials.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

```json
"https://youtube.com"
```

## CatalogList

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId, draft or workspace is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| draft | no | object | {"properties":{"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["draftId","workspaceId","provider"]} |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
    "workspaceId": "example"
  }
}
```

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

```json
  "type": "events"
}
```

```json
}
```

| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

| generation | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |

```json
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 8,
  "before": 6,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "turnOrdinal": 1,
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| to | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"model":{"type":"string"}},"required":["provider"]} |
| lossy | no | boolean |  |

```json
  }
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 7,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "tool": "ace_read_handoff",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 3,
  "origin": {
    "provider": "cursor"
  },
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 4,
  "version": 1
}
```

| cwd | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |
| env | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |

| shortLabel | no | [AccountBadgeInput.json](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json) |  |
| method | yes | [AccountLoginMethod.json](schema/AccountLoginMethod.json) |  |
| upstream | no | [ApiKeyUpstream.json](schema/ApiKeyUpstream.json) |  |

```json
}
```

## ProviderConfiguration

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderConfiguration.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| provider | yes | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| instance | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"} |
| enabled | no | boolean |  |
| defaultModel | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$","x-ace-constraint":"The pseudo model ID default is not accepted; choose a concrete model ID."},{"type":"null"}]} |
| binaryPath | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096,"pattern":"^(?:\\/\|[A-Za-z]:[\\\\/]).*$","x-ace-constraint":"Absolute executable path with no control bytes."} |
| customModels | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"displayName":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"\\S"}},"required":["id","displayName"]},"x-ace-constraint":"Custom model IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownModels | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| hiddenGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| shownGroups | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| favourites | no | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256,"pattern":"^[^\\s\\x00-\\x1f\\x7f]+$"},"uniqueItems":true,"x-ace-constraint":"IDs must be unique."} |
| showOnlyFavourites | no | boolean |  |
| hideDeprecated | no | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "defaultModel": null,
  "enabled": false,
  "favourites": [],
  "hiddenModels": [],
  "instance": "~yz}~~}",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "shownModels": []
}
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "local:agent",
  "env": {},
  "homeDir": "/example/home",
  "homeStrategy": "default_cli",
  "id": "installed:default",
  "installationId": "installed",
  "instanceId": "installed:default",
  "label": "Local agent",
  "provider": "acp"
}
```

| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| url | no | intersection | {"allOf":[{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":8192,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. | userCode | no | string | {"pattern":"^[A-Z0-9]{4,5}-[A-Z0-9]{4,5}$"} |
| prompt | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| choices | no | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[a-z0-9_-]{1,64}$"},"label":{"type":"string","maxLength":128}},"required":["id","label"],"additionalProperties":false}} |

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |
| fingerprint | yes | [ReviewFingerprint.json](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

| reply | no | [ReviewReply.json](schema/ReviewReply.json) |  |
| sessions | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewSession.json"}} |
| comments | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewComment.json"}} |
| replies | no | array | {"maxItems":20,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ReviewReply.json"}} |
| nextCursor | no | string |  |
| intentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |

Example:

```json
{
  "intentId": "example",
  "nextCursor": "example",
  "sessions": []
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| after | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| noFinalNewline | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": [],
  "before": [],
  "lines": [
    "example"
  ],
  "noFinalNewline": true
}
```

```json
    },

      "kind": "commit",

    },

    "worktree": "example"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

```json

  "end": 1,
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

## ScreenCapabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenCapabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| permissionEvents | no | boolean |  |
| maxSessions | no | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| platform | yes | ["macos","windows","linux-x11","linux-wayland"] |  |
| capture | yes | object | {"properties":{"windows":{"type":"boolean"},"displays":{"type":"boolean"},"changeDriven":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["windows","displays","changeDriven"]} |
| input | yes | object | {"properties":{"pointer":{"type":"boolean"},"keyboard":{"type":"boolean"},"scroll":{"type":"boolean"},"text":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["pointer","keyboard","scroll","text"]} |

## ScreenHelperError

[JSON Schema](schema/ScreenHelperError.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| code | yes | ["forbidden","permission_denied","approval_required","screen_disabled","denied","read_only","target_gone","not_supported","bounds","busy","timeout","internal","target_busy","foreground_required","focus_changed","window_minimized","window_offscreen","secure_input_required","clipboard_changed","window_ambiguous","key_unsupported","modifier_unsupported","no_key_window","delivery_unconfirmed"] |  |
| message | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| phase | no | ["rejected-before-dispatch","dispatched","partial"] |  |
| candidates | no | array | {"maxItems":2048,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"windowId":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"bundleId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenBundle.json"},"title":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},"bounds":{"type":"object","properties":{"x":{"type":"number"},"y":{"type":"number"},"w":{"type":"number"},"h":{"type":"number"}},"required":["x","y","w","h"]}},"required":["windowId","title"]}} |

Example:

```json

  "code": "bounds",
  "message": "example"
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":9,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "provider": "pi",
  "status": "unresponsive",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

| content | yes | array | {"maxItems":16,"items":{"oneOf":[{"type":"object","properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"text"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":32768}},"required":["type","text"]},{"type":"object","properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"image"},"attachment":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Attachment.json"}},"required":["type","attachment"]}]}} |
| structuredContent | no | any JSON value |  |
| durationMs | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| target | no | object | {"properties":{"bundleId":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"windowId":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"displayName":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["bundleId","displayName"]} |
| mode | no | ["background","foreground"] |  |
| scale | no | number | {"exclusiveMinimum":0} |
| size | no | object | {"properties":{"width":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":16384},"height":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":16384}},"required":["width","height"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "content": [],

  "mode": "background",
  "scale": 6,
  "structuredContent": null,

    "bundleId": "example",
    "displayName": "example"
  }
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

| timezone | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

| priceAsOf | no | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| priceSources | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}} |

| accounts | no | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountSummary.json"}} |
